Surah: 83. Follow the brief below (surah_map.md) exactly. The evidence is text.md (the surah), dictionary.md (every root of the surah's words, each branch with the classical dictionaries' own phrases), channels.md and hft.md (both are earlier readers' proposals: ignore their judgements (grades, strength or confidence labels, reading types, statements of what a reading may or may not do) and make your own) and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Return only the map as your final message.

===== _commentary/v16/prompts/r2/surah_map.md =====
Read the surah as one text and write its map of image chains: the lexical images that run through several of
its ayat and join them into one scene, process or movement. A later writer will read one ayah at a time, with
only that ayah's own dictionary. The map lets that writer hear what the ayah's words carry in the surah as a
whole, including senses whose evidence sits under the words of other ayat.

Your evidence is the surah text, the dictionary of every root in the surah (each branch with the classical
dictionaries' own phrases), earlier readers' channel review (channels.md) and activation hypotheses (hft.md),
and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Where a member or passage comes from memory rather than from
the dictionary or the text, say so. Do not use tools, delegate, browse or inspect files.

The channel review and the HFT records are proposals by earlier readers. Ignore their judgements: grades,
strength or confidence labels, reading types, words such as "surprising", "exploratory" or "latent", and every
statement of what a reading may or may not do. Make your own judgement from the surah's words, the dictionary
phrases and the Quran. Do not rediscover what they already assembled: start from their chains, test each one
against the dictionary phrases and the text, and join, extend, split or correct them. Where their wording
abstracts a member, go back to the dictionary phrase and name what it actually says.

A chain belongs on the map when its members are senses the dictionary attests for words that stand in the
surah, and together they make one image or process that the surah's wording or sequence lets a listener hear.
A member need not be the sense that translates its word in its own ayah; a chain is heard across the surah,
not in one word. Say plainly when a member is a rare sense or is attested only inside a fixed expression, and
keep it. When the dictionary itself joins two of the surah's words in one phrase, quote that phrase: it is
the strongest evidence a chain can have. Keep a scene at the level of its objects, their parts and their
operation. When proposals share members, do not fold one into another's more abstract function unless nothing
concrete is lost. Carry every chain that meets this test, however unusual; leave out proposals that do not.

Write the map in English, with Arabic quoted exactly (surah wording from the text, dictionary phrases from the
dictionary). It is working material for the writer, not commentary prose.

1. `## Chains`. For each chain, a `###` heading naming the image. One paragraph on what the image is and how it
   moves through the surah. Then its members, one line each: the ayah ref, the word as it stands in the text,
   the root and branch id, the dictionary's own phrase quoted exactly, and what this member contributes to the
   image. Add Quran passages outside the surah, with exact refs, where they stage or confirm the chain.
2. `## Ayat`. For each ayah in order: which chains it opens, advances or completes, and what its words add to
   each.
3. `## Not carried`. Each channel subchannel and each HFT record you did not carry into a chain, one line each:
   its name and why.

No ranking and no labels of strength or confidence. No list of what a writer must include. There is no length
target and no required number of chains or members.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s083/surah.r2/text.md =====
# Surah 83

- 83:1 وَيْلٌۭ لِّلْمُطَفِّفِينَ
- 83:2 ٱلَّذِينَ إِذَا ٱكْتَالُوا۟ عَلَى ٱلنَّاسِ يَسْتَوْفُونَ
- 83:3 وَإِذَا كَالُوهُمْ أَو وَّزَنُوهُمْ يُخْسِرُونَ
- 83:4 أَلَا يَظُنُّ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ أَنَّهُم مَّبْعُوثُونَ
- 83:5 لِيَوْمٍ عَظِيمٍۢ
- 83:6 يَوْمَ يَقُومُ ٱلنَّاسُ لِرَبِّ ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ
- 83:7 كَلَّآ إِنَّ كِتَٰبَ ٱلْفُجَّارِ لَفِى سِجِّينٍۢ
- 83:8 وَمَآ أَدْرَىٰكَ مَا سِجِّينٌۭ
- 83:9 كِتَٰبٌۭ مَّرْقُومٌۭ
- 83:10 وَيْلٌۭ يَوْمَئِذٍۢ لِّلْمُكَذِّبِينَ
- 83:11 ٱلَّذِينَ يُكَذِّبُونَ بِيَوْمِ ٱلدِّينِ
- 83:12 وَمَا يُكَذِّبُ بِهِۦٓ إِلَّا كُلُّ مُعْتَدٍ أَثِيمٍ
- 83:13 إِذَا تُتْلَىٰ عَلَيْهِ ءَايَٰتُنَا قَالَ أَسَٰطِيرُ ٱلْأَوَّلِينَ
- 83:14 كَلَّا ۖ بَلْ ۜ رَانَ عَلَىٰ قُلُوبِهِم مَّا كَانُوا۟ يَكْسِبُونَ
- 83:15 كَلَّآ إِنَّهُمْ عَن رَّبِّهِمْ يَوْمَئِذٍۢ لَّمَحْجُوبُونَ
- 83:16 ثُمَّ إِنَّهُمْ لَصَالُوا۟ ٱلْجَحِيمِ
- 83:17 ثُمَّ يُقَالُ هَٰذَا ٱلَّذِى كُنتُم بِهِۦ تُكَذِّبُونَ
- 83:18 كَلَّآ إِنَّ كِتَٰبَ ٱلْأَبْرَارِ لَفِى عِلِّيِّينَ
- 83:19 وَمَآ أَدْرَىٰكَ مَا عِلِّيُّونَ
- 83:20 كِتَٰبٌۭ مَّرْقُومٌۭ
- 83:21 يَشْهَدُهُ ٱلْمُقَرَّبُونَ
- 83:22 إِنَّ ٱلْأَبْرَارَ لَفِى نَعِيمٍ
- 83:23 عَلَى ٱلْأَرَآئِكِ يَنظُرُونَ
- 83:24 تَعْرِفُ فِى وُجُوهِهِمْ نَضْرَةَ ٱلنَّعِيمِ
- 83:25 يُسْقَوْنَ مِن رَّحِيقٍۢ مَّخْتُومٍ
- 83:26 خِتَٰمُهُۥ مِسْكٌۭ ۚ وَفِى ذَٰلِكَ فَلْيَتَنَافَسِ ٱلْمُتَنَٰفِسُونَ
- 83:27 وَمِزَاجُهُۥ مِن تَسْنِيمٍ
- 83:28 عَيْنًۭا يَشْرَبُ بِهَا ٱلْمُقَرَّبُونَ
- 83:29 إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ أَجْرَمُوا۟ كَانُوا۟ مِنَ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ يَضْحَكُونَ
- 83:30 وَإِذَا مَرُّوا۟ بِهِمْ يَتَغَامَزُونَ
- 83:31 وَإِذَا ٱنقَلَبُوٓا۟ إِلَىٰٓ أَهْلِهِمُ ٱنقَلَبُوا۟ فَكِهِينَ
- 83:32 وَإِذَا رَأَوْهُمْ قَالُوٓا۟ إِنَّ هَٰٓؤُلَآءِ لَضَآلُّونَ
- 83:33 وَمَآ أُرْسِلُوا۟ عَلَيْهِمْ حَٰفِظِينَ
- 83:34 فَٱلْيَوْمَ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ مِنَ ٱلْكُفَّارِ يَضْحَكُونَ
- 83:35 عَلَى ٱلْأَرَآئِكِ يَنظُرُونَ
- 83:36 هَلْ ثُوِّبَ ٱلْكُفَّارُ مَا كَانُوا۟ يَفْعَلُونَ


===== _commentary/v16/work/s083/surah.r2/dictionary.md =====
# Dictionary: every branch of every focus root

Every root of the surah's words, each once, headed by the ayah and word where it first occurs; it also serves the later words listed with it.
One entry per branch: a Turkish label and Turkish glosses of its attested senses, then the classical dictionaries' own phrases with their source tags (ayn, sihah, tahdhib, maqayis, mufradat, jamhara). [kalıp] marks a sense the dictionaries attest only inside a fixed expression.
Identity roots come from the quran-data gateway. Word-scoped alternatives are cited analyses of this
exact word. **Echo roots** are observed but withheld mappings: sound-family candidates, not identity.

## ر ب ب (root_000532): 83:6 لِرَبِّ, 83:15 رَّبِّهِمْ

  الرب: الله تبارك وتعالى؛ ورب كل شيء مالكه (jamhara); رب كل شئ: مالكه؛ وقد قالوه في الجاهلية للملك؛ رببت القوم: سستهم (sihah); يكون الرب: المالك؛ ويكون الرب: السيد المطاع؛ ويكون الرب: المصلح (tahdhib); الرب مصدر مستعار للفاعل؛ لا يقال الرب مطلقا إلا لله؛ رب الدار ورب الفرس (mufradat); فالرب المالك والخالق والصاحب؛ والله جل ثناؤه الرب (maqayis)

  الرباني: المتأله العارف بالله تعالى (sihah); الرباني: العالم؛ العلماء بالحلال والحرام؛ حكماء علماء؛ العالم المعلم الذي يغذو الناس بصغار العلوم (tahdhib); الرباني... يرب العلم؛ يرب نفسه بالعلم؛ منسوب إلى الرب (mufradat); الربي العارف بالرب (maqayis)

  تأمل الشيء ومعاينته؛ نظرت إلى الشيء إذا عاينته (maqayis)؛ النظر تأمل الشئ بالعين (sihah)؛ نظر العين ونظر القلب؛ نظرت في الأمر احتمل أن يكون تفكرا وتدبرا بالقلب (tahdhib)؛ تقليب البصر والبصيرة لإدراك الشيء ورؤيته؛ التأمل والفحص؛ المعرفة الحاصلة بعد الفحص؛ مشاهدون؛ تعتبرون (mufradat)
- **B002** bekleme veya süre tanıma — onu beklemek · bekleme · durup beklemek · ona süre verip ertelemek · ondan ek süre istemek · bekleyip geleceğini gözetmek · erteleme ve geciktirme · süre verme ve erteleme · bekle · önce Tanrı'dan, sonra senden iyilik umarım · kendisinden iyilik umulan
  نظرته أي انتظرته؛ ينظر إلى الوقت الذي يأتي فيه (maqayis)؛ النظر الانتظار؛ النظرة التأخير؛ أنظرته أي أخرته؛ استنظره أي استمهله (sihah)؛ إنما أنظر إلى الله ثم إليك أي أتوقع فضل الله ثم فضلك؛ أنظرني أي انتظرني قليلا؛ أمهلته؛ النظرة إنظار (tahdhib)؛ النظر الانتظار؛ نظرته وانتظرته وأنظرته أي أخرته (mufradat)
- **B003** görüş doğrultusunda karşı karşıya olma [kalıp] — birbirini görebilen komşu topluluk · evim onun evine bakar ve karşısındadır · dağ yol üzerinde karşına çıktı
  حي حلال نظر متجاورون ينظر بعضهم إلى بعض (maqayis)؛ حي حلال ونظر أي متجاورون يرى بعضهم بعضا؛ داري تنظر إلى دار فلان؛ دورنا تناظر أي تقابل؛ فنظر إليك الجبل (sihah)؛ داري تنظر إلى دار فلان ودورنا تناظر إذا كانت متحاذية (tahdhib)؛ حي نظر أي متجاورون يرى بعضهم بعضا (mufradat)
- **B004** bakıldığında görülen dış görünüş — toprak bitkisini gösterdi · bakıldığında görülen dış görünüş · güzel dış görünüş · bakılması ve dinlenmesi hoş bir durumda
  نظرت الأرض أرت نباتها (maqayis)؛ منظره خير من مخبره؛ حسنة المنظر والمنظرة (sihah)؛ المنظرة منظر الرجل إذا نظرت إليه فأعجبك أو ساءك؛ ذو منظرة بلا مخبرة؛ المنظر الشيء الذي يعجب الناظر إذا نظر إليه فسره؛ في منظر ومستمع (tahdhib)
- **B005** zamanın vurup yok etmesi [kalıp] — zaman onları vurup yok etti
  نظر الدهر إلى بني فلان فأهلكهم (maqayis;sihah)؛ نظر الدهر إليهم فابتهل؛ خانهم فأهلكهم (mufradat)
- **B006** eş veya denk karşılık — eş, benzer veya denk karşılık · birbirine benzeyen eşler · Tanrı'nın kitabına hiçbir şeyi denk tutma · ikişer ikişer sayılan develer
  هذا نظير هذا؛ إذا نظر إليه وإلى نظيره كانا سواء (maqayis)؛ نظير الشئ مثله؛ النظر والنظير بمعنى واحد مثل الند والنديد؛ نظائر (sihah)؛ فلان نظيرك أي مثلك؛ النظائر لاشتباه بعضها ببعض؛ لا تجعل شيئا نظيرا (tahdhib)؛ النظير المثيل وأصله المناظر (mufradat)
- **B007** özel adlandırma kümesi — hızlı bir bakış · bakıştan doğan heybet · onda solgunluk, çirkinlik veya kusur var · görünmeyen varlıkların kem gözünden etkilenmiş · kem gözden zarar görmüş kişi
  به نظرة أي شحوب (maqayis)؛ النظرة عين الجن؛ رجل فيه نظرة أي شحوب (sihah)؛ النظرة اللمحة بالعجلة؛ النظرة الهيبة؛ فيه نظرة أي شحوب؛ النظرة الشنعة والقبح؛ فيه نظرة أي قبح؛ بها إصابة عين من نظر الجن (tahdhib)؛ به نظرة؛ من أعين الجن نظرة (mufradat)
- **B008** biçime bağlı adlandırmalar — göz bebeği veya gözdeki küçük siyah bölüm · göz · gözyaşı yolunda burnun iki yanındaki damarlar
  الناظر في المقلة السواد الأصغر؛ يقال للعين الناظرة؛ الناظران عرقان في مجرى الدمع على الأنف من جانبيه (sihah)؛ ناظر العين النقطة السوداء الصافية؛ الناظر في العين كالمرآة؛ الناظران عرقان مكتنفا الأنف؛ هما عرقان في مجرى الدمع (tahdhib)
- **B009** biçime bağlı adlandırmalar — bağ bekçisi · koruyucu veya inceleme için gönderilen görevli · yüksek gözetleme yeri · suçsuzluğundan gözünü kaçırmadan bakan
  الناطر والناطور حافظ الكرم؛ الناظر الحافظ؛ المنظرة المرقبة (sihah)؛ المنظرة موضع في رأس جبل فيه رقيب ينظر العدو ويحرسه؛ شديد الناظر إذا كان بريئا من التهمة؛ بعث ناظرا (tahdhib)
- **B010** karşılıklı tartışıp inceleme — karşılıklı tartışıp inceleme · araştırma ve kanıta dayalı inceleme
  ناظره من المناظرة (sihah)؛ المناظرة أن تناظر أخاك في أمر إذا نظرتما فيه معا كيف تأتيانه؛ نظيرك أيضا الذي يناظرك وتناظره (tahdhib)؛ المناظرة المباحثة والمباراة في النظر واستحضار كل ما يراه ببصيرته؛ النظر البحث (mufradat)
- **B011** merhametle iyilik yöneltme — merhamet · Tanrı kullarına iyilik etti ve iyiliklerini bolca verdi
  النظرة الرحمة (tahdhib)؛ نظر الله تعالى إلى عباده هو إحسانه إليهم وإفاضة نعمه عليهم (mufradat)
- **B012** bakıp da görememe ve kavrayamama [kalıp] — bakıyorlar ama göremiyor ve kavrayamıyorlar
  يستعمل النظر في التحير في الأمور؛ ينظرون إليك وهم لا يبصرون؛ نظر عن تحير دال على قلة الغناء (mufradat)

## ع ر ف (root_001002): 83:24 تَعْرِفُ

- **B001** kalıp sözlerde peş peşe gelme veya üst üste yığılma [kalıp] — birbiri ardından peş peşe gelme · at yelesi gibi art arda gönderilenler · katları üst üste konmuş yemek · çokluktan dolayı üst üste yığmak
  تتابع الشيء متصلا بعضه ببعض (maqayis)؛ جاءت القطا عرفا عرفا (maqayis;mufradat)؛ المرسلات عرفا متتابعة كعرف الفرس (sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ خزير معرف بعضه على بعض (tahdhib)
- **B002** bir şeyin üzerindeki belirgin tepe, sırt veya üst çıkıntı — yele, ibik ya da belirgin yükselti · atın yelesini kırpmak · iki düzlük arasındaki yüksekçe arazi veya kum sırtı · yüksek yerler ya da yüksek bir duvar · denizin dalgalarının yükselmesi veya sıvının köpüklenmesi
  العرف عرف الفرس (maqayis;sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ العرفة أرض منقادة مرتفعة (maqayis)؛ عرفت الفرس أي جززت عرفه (sihah)؛ الأعراف جمع عرف وهو كل عال مرتفع (tahdhib)؛ العرف والعرف الرمل المرتفع (sihah)؛ أعراف الرياح والسحاب أوائلها وأعاليها (tahdhib)
- **B003** bir iz veya belirti üzerinden tanıyıp ayırt etme — iz veya belirti üzerinden tanıma ve ayırt etme · bir şeyi bilip başkalarından ayırt etmek · bilinen ve ayırt edilmiş, bilinmez olmayan · topluluktakilerin birbirini tanıması · bildirme ve bilinir duruma getirme · birinin elindekini araştırıp öğrenmek · anlatının bir bölümünü bildirip bir bölümünü bırakmak · yerlerini önceden betimleyip tanınır kılmak
  عرف فلان فلانا عرفانا ومعرفة (maqayis)؛ عرفت الشيء معرفة وعرفانا (ayn;sihah;tahdhib)؛ المعرفة والعرفان إدراك الشيء بتفكر وتدبر لأثره (mufradat)؛ تعارف القوم أي عرف بعضهم بعضا (sihah;mufradat)؛ التعريف الإعلام (sihah)؛ تعرفت ما عند فلان أي تطلبت حتى عرفت (sihah)
- **B004** koku; ayrıca güzel kokulu duruma getirme — hoş ya da kötü olabilen koku · bir şeyi güzel kokulu duruma getirme · güzel kokuyla işlenmiş veya süslenmiş yemek · onlar için güzel kokulu ve süslü duruma getirmek · güzel kokuyu çok kullanmak ya da kullanmayı bırakmak
  العرف وهي الرائحة الطيبة (maqayis)؛ العرف الريح طيبة كانت أو منتنة (sihah)؛ العرف الرائحة تكون طيبة وغير طيبة (tahdhib)؛ التعريف التطييب (sihah)؛ عرفها لهم أي طيبها وزينها (mufradat)
- **B005** iyi sayılan ve benimsenen davranış veya iyilik — iyilik ve başkasına iyi davranma · düşünce veya bağlayıcı ölçülerce iyi sayılan davranış · bilinen ve beğenilen iş
  العرف المعروف (maqayis;ayn;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ المعروف ضد المنكر والعرف ضد النكر (sihah)؛ كل ما تعرفه النفس من الخير وتبسأ به وتطمئن إليه (tahdhib)؛ اسم لكل فعل يعرف بالعقل أو الشرع حسنه (mufradat)
- **B006** topluluğu tanıyan ve işlerini gözeten görevli — topluluğun işlerini bilen gözetmen veya temsilci · topluluk gözetmenliği görevi veya yetkisi
  العريف القيم بأمر قوم (maqayis;ayn)؛ العريف النقيب وهو دون الرئيس (sihah)؛ عريف القوم سيدهم (tahdhib)؛ العريف بمن يعرف الناس ويعرفهم (mufradat)
- **B007** belirli ibadet yeri, onun günü ve orada bulunup durma — belirli bir ibadet bölgesinin adı · insanların o ibadet yerinde durduğu belirli gün · belirli ibadet yerinde bulunup durma
  عرفات سميت بذلك لأن آدم وحواء تعارفا بها (maqayis)؛ يوم عرفة موقف الناس بعرفات (ayn)؛ عرفات موضع (sihah)؛ عرف الناس إذا شهدوا عرفة وهو المعرف للموقف بعرفات (tahdhib)؛ عرفات اسم لبقعة مخصوصة (mufradat)
- **B008** tanıyanı duyuruyla arama veya sorarak haber öğrenme [kalıp] — kayıp veya bulunan şeyi tanıyanı duyuruyla arama · insanlara bir haber öğrenmek için sormak · bulunan şeyi özelliklerinden tanıyan kişi
  التعريف تعريف الضالة واللقطة أن يقول من يعرف هذا (maqayis)؛ التعريف أن تصيب شيئا فتعرفه إذا ناديت من يعرف هذا (ayn)؛ التعريف إنشاد الضالة (sihah)؛ اعترفت القوم سألتهم (sihah;tahdhib)؛ من يعترفها فمعناه معرفته إياها بصفتها (tahdhib)
- **B009** bir şeyi veya suçu açıkça kabul edip üstlenme — bir şeyi veya suçu açıkça kabul etme · kişinin kendi suçunu kabul etmesi · kimsenin beni yenebileceğini kabul etmem
  اعترف بالشيء إذا أقر (maqayis)؛ الاعتراف الإقرار بالذنب والذل والمهانة والرضى به (ayn)؛ المعرف الاسم من الاعتراف وله علي ألف عرفا أي اعترافا (sihah)؛ عرف الرجل ذنبه إذا أقر به (tahdhib)؛ اعترف فلان إذا ذل وانقاد (tahdhib)
- **B010** yüklenilen duruma dayanıp içten sakinleşme — üzerine yüklenene dayanıp sakinleşen ruh · sıkıntı karşısında sabırlı kişi · sabır ve dayanma
  النفس عروف إذا حملت على أمر فباءت به أي اطمأنت (maqayis)؛ النفس عروف إذا حملت على أمر بسأت به أي اطمأنت (ayn)؛ العارف الصبور والعروف مثله (sihah)؛ رجل عارف أي صبور ونفس عروف صبور (tahdhib)؛ العرف بالكسر الصبر (tahdhib)
- **B011** avuç içinin açık bölümünde çıkan yara veya çıban — avuç içinin açık renkli bölümünde çıkan yara
  العرفة قرحة تخرج في بياض الكف (sihah;tahdhib)؛ عرف الرجال فهو معروف أي خرجت به تلك القرحة (sihah)
- **B012** gizli haber iddiacısı, hekim veya işinin uzmanı — gizli haber bildiğini öne süren kişi, hekim veya işinin uzmanı
  العراف الكاهن والطبيب (sihah)؛ للحازي عراف وللقناقن عراف وللطبيب عراف (tahdhib)؛ العراف كالكاهن إلا أن العراف يختص بمن يخبر بالأحوال المستقبلة (mufradat)
- **B013** yüzü veya araziyi tanıtan belirgin görünür bölümler — yüzler ve yüzün görünen bölümleri · arazinin tanınan ve görünür kısımları
  امرأة حسنة المعارف أي الوجه وما يظهر منها (sihah)؛ المعارف الوجوه والمعرف واحد (tahdhib)؛ معارف الأرض ما عرف منها (tahdhib)؛ أصبت عرفه أي خده (mufradat)
- **B014** kötülüğe hazırlanıp sert ve saldırgan bir tavır alma [kalıp] — kötülüğe hazırlanıp sert bir tavır almak
  اعرورف الرجل أي تهيأ للشر (sihah)؛ اعرورف فلان للشر كقولك اجثأل وتشزن (tahdhib)

## و ج ه (root_001630): 83:24 وُجُوهِهِمْ

- **B001** yüz ve bir şeyin öne bakan yanı — yüz; bir şeyin öne bakan veya görünen yanı · kötü bir yüz ifadesiyle bakmak
  الوجه مستقبل لكل شيء (maqayis); الوجه مستقبل كل شيء (ayn;tahdhib); وجه الإنسان وغيره معروف (jamhara); الوجه معروف (sihah); أصل الوجه الجارحة (mufradat)
- **B002** yön ve hedef; o yöne sevk etme veya yolu belli etme — yön, taraf · yönelinen yön veya hedef · bir şeyi belirli bir yöne çevirmek veya göndermek · tek bir yöne çevrilmiş · bir şeye doğru yönelmek · rüzgarın çakılı bir yöne sürüklemesi · yolu yürüyerek izini belirginleştirmek · perdeyi yırtacak bir yöne gitmek veya perdeyi yerinden kaldırmak
  الوجهة كل موضع استقبلته (maqayis); الجهة النحو (ayn;tahdhib); الوجهة القبلة وشبهها (ayn;tahdhib); ضل وجهة أمره إذا ضل قصده (jamhara); وجهته في حاجة ووجهت وجهي لله وتوجهت نحوك وإليك (sihah); وجهت الريح الحصا إذا ساقته ووجهوا للناس الطريق إذا وطئوه وسلكوه (tahdhib); للمقصد جهة ووجهة (mufradat)
- **B003** karşı karşıya gelme ve doğrudan yüzüne söyleme — birinin karşısına çıkmak, onunla yüz yüze gelmek · karşılaşma, yüzleşme · karşında, tam karşı tarafta
  واجهت فلانا جعلت وجهي تلقاء وجهه (maqayis;mufradat); الوجاه والتجاه ما استقبل شيء شيئا (ayn;tahdhib); المواجهة استقبالك الرجل بكلام (ayn;tahdhib); واجهت الرجل بكلام حسن أو قبيح (jamhara); المواجهة المقابلة وقعدت وجاهك أي قبالتك (sihah)
- **B004** yüzün varlığın kendisini temsil etmesi — 
  ربما عبر عن الذات بالوجه (maqayis); قيل ذاته وكل شيء هالك إلا هو (mufradat)
- **B005** amaç edinip yönelme; ibadette içtenlikle bağlanma — 
  وجهي إليك (maqayis); ضل وجهة أمره إذا ضل قصده (jamhara); وجهت وجهي لله سبحانه (sihah); الوجه الذي يؤتى منه وما أريد به الله وأخلصوا العبادة لله وأسلمت وجهي لله (mufradat)
- **B006** toplumsal itibar, yüksek mevki ve önde gelen kişi — topluluğun önderi · yerleşimin ileri gelenleri · itibarlı, yüksek mevkili · toplumsal itibar ve mevki · itibarlı ve yüksek mevkili olmak · onu itibarlı ve yüksek mevkili kılmak
  وجيه بين الجاه والجاه مقلوب (maqayis); وجوه القوم سادتهم ورجل وجيه عند السلطان (jamhara); صار وجيها أي ذا جاه وقدر ووجوه البلد أشرافه (sihah); جاه فيهم أي منزلة وقدر (tahdhib); فلان وجه القوم وفلان وجيه ذو جاه (mufradat)
- **B007** günün başı, ilk saatleri [kalıp] — günün başı, ilk saatleri
  وجه النهار أوله (jamhara); أتيته بوجه نهار وشباب نهار وصدر نهار أي في أوله (tahdhib); وجه النهار أي صدر النهار (mufradat)
- **B008** sözün veya işin doğru yönü ve ona uygun düzenleme — sözün amaçlanan yönü · doğru görüş · aklına bir görüş gelmek · bir şeyi doğru yolundan saptırmak · işi gerektiği gibi düzenleyip her şeyi yerine koymak · hiçbir işi doğru yapamayan ahmak; ayrıca tuvaletini yapmayı bile beceremeyen kişi
  وجه الكلام السبيل التي تقصدها به وصرفت الشيء عن وجهه أي عن سننه (jamhara); هذا وجه الرأي أي هو الرأي نفسه وأحمق ما يتوجه (sihah); دبر الأمر على وجهه الذي ينبغي وأحمق ما يتوجه أي ما يحسن أن يأتي الغائط (tahdhib); أحمق ما يتوجه أي لا يستقيم في أمر من الأمور (mufradat)
- **B009** yaşlanıp ömrünün son dönemine girmek — yaşlanıp ömrünün son dönemine girmek
  توجه الشيخ ولى وأدبر (maqayis); توجه الشيخ إذا ولى وكبر (sihah); إذا كبر سنه قد توجه (tahdhib)
- **B010** doğumda ellerin veya ön ayakların önce çıkması — elleri veya ön ayakları önce çıkan yavru · yavruyu elleri veya ön ayakları önce çıkacak biçimde doğurmak
  للمهر إذا خرجت يداه من الرحم وجيه (maqayis); للولد إذا خرجت يداه من الرحم أولا وجيه (sihah); أوجهت به أمه حين ولدته إذا خرج يداه أولا (tahdhib)
- **B011** kurucu uzun ünlü ile ana uyak harfi arasındaki harf — kurucu uzun ünlü ile ana uyak harfi arasındaki harf
  التوجيه هو الحرف الذي بين ألف التأسيس وبين القافية (sihah); الصاد توجيه بين التأسيس والقافية (tahdhib); التوجيه في الشعر الحرف الذي بين ألف التأسيس وحرف الروي (mufradat)
- **B012** hıyar veya kavunun altını kazıp yana yatırma — hıyar veya kavunun altını kazıp yana yatırma

  أصل واحد يدل على الانبعاث والامتداد (maqayis)؛ أرسلت فلانا في رسالة والمرسلات الرياح ويقال الملائكة (sihah)؛ إرسال الله أنبياءه وإرسال الشياطين تخليتهم وإياهم (tahdhib)؛ الإرسال يقابل الإمساك (mufradat)
- **B002** haber taşıyıcısı veya taşınan haber — elçi veya haberci · taşınan ileti veya haber · ileti veya taşınan haber · iletiler veya taşınan haberler · elçiler veya haberciler
  الرسول معروف (maqayis)؛ الرسول بمعنى الرسالة والرسائل جمع الرسالة (ayn)؛ أرسلت فلانا في رسالة فهو مرسل ورسول والرسول أيضا الرسالة (sihah)؛ الرسول معناه الذي يتابع أخبار الذي بعثه (tahdhib)؛ الرسول يقال للقول المتحمل وتارة لمتحمل القول والرسالة (mufradat)

- **B004** acele etmeden ölçülü ilerleme — Acele etme; yavaş ve sakin ol · işte veya konuşmada sakin, ağırbaşlı ve temkinli davranma · metni acele etmeden açık seçik okuma
  على رسلك أي على هينتك (maqayis)؛ تكلم على رسلك والترسل في الأمر والمنطق كالتمهل والتوقر والتثبت (ayn)؛ على رسلك أي اتئد فيه وترسل في قراءته (sihah)؛ الترسل من الرسل في الأمور والمنطق كالتمهل والتوقر والتثبت والترسيل التحقيق بلا عجلة (tahdhib)؛ على رسلك إذا أمرته بالرفق (mufradat)
- **B005** peş peşe gelen topluluklar — deve, koyun veya başka varlıklardan oluşan sürü · gruplar halinde, birbirinin ardından
  الرسل ما أرسل من الغنم إلى الرعي وجاء القوم أرسالا يتبع بعضهم بعضا (maqayis)؛ الرسل القطيع من كل شيء وجمعه أرسال (ayn)؛ الرسل القطيع من الإبل والغنم وجاءت الخيل أرسالا قطيعا قطيعا (sihah)؛ جاءت الإبل أرسالا رسل بعد رسل والرسل قطيع من الإبل (tahdhib)؛ جاءوا أرسالا أي متتابعين (mufradat)
- **B006** bol ve sürekli gelen süt — süt; özellikle bol ve sürekli gelen süt · hayvanlarından süt elde eder duruma gelmek
  الرِّسل اللبن لأنه يترسل من الضرع (maqayis)؛ والرسل اللبن (ayn)؛ والرسل أيضا اللبن وقد أرسل القوم أي صار لهم اللبن (sihah)؛ كثر الرسل العام أي كثر اللبن (tahdhib)؛ الرسل اللبن الكثير المتتابع الدر (mufradat)
- **B007** ısınıp güvenerek açılma — birine veya bir şeye ısınıp güvenmek · sana güvenip yanında rahat davranan kimse

- **B008** karşılıklı iletişim ve eşlik — karşılıklı haberleşmek veya birbirine ayak uydurmak · atışmada veya başka bir uğraşta eşlik eden kişi · şarkıda veya işte bir öncekinin ardından giden eşlikçi
  رسيل الرجل الذي يقف معه في نضال أو غيره (maqayis)؛ راسله مراسلة فهو مراسل ورسيل الرجل الذي يراسله في نضال أو غيره (sihah)؛ العرب تسمي المراسل في الغناء والعمل المتالي (tahdhib)
- **B009** taliplerin haber gönderdiği dul veya ayrılmak üzere olan kadın [kalıp] — eşi ölmüş, boşanmış veya ayrılmak üzere olduğu için taliplerin haber gönderdiği kadın
  المرأة المراسل التي مات بعلها فالخطاب يراسلونها (maqayis)؛ امرأة مراسل كان لها زوج والخطاب يراسلونها الخطبة (ayn)؛ امرأة مراسل يموت زوجها أو أحست منه أنه يريد تطليقها (sihah)؛ امرأة مراسل وهي التي مات عنها زوجها أو طلقها (tahdhib)
- **B010** rahatlık ve gönül hoşluğuyla verme [kalıp] — sıkıntısında ve rahatlığında; gönül hoşluğuyla verirken
  النجدة الشدة والرسل الرخاء (maqayis)؛ في نجدتها ورسلها يريد الشدة والرخاء (sihah)؛ إلا من أعطى في رسلها أي بطيب نفس منه (tahdhib)
- **B011** özel adlandırma kümesi — kısa ok · iki damar · belirli bir topluluğun damızlık erkek devesi · aktarım zinciri kesintili söz · boncuklu kolye · başını henüz örtmeyen küçük kız

## ح ف ظ (root_000342): 83:33 حَٰفِظِينَ

- **B001** koruyup gözetme — koruyup gözetme ve bakımını üstlenme · bir şeyi koruyup kolladı · bir şeyi koruyan ya da korumakla görevlendirilen kimse · insanların yaptıklarını sayıp yazan melekler · onu yalnız kendisi için sakladı · onu korumasını istedi ve güvenip kendisine teslim etti · koruma altında, kaybolmadan ve değişmeden saklanmış · gözetip başında durma
  أصل واحد يدل على مراعاة الشيء (maqayis)؛ الحفيظ الموكل بالشيء يحفظه (ayn;tahdhib)؛ حفظت الشيء حفظا (jamhara)؛ حفظت الشئ حفظا أي حرسته (sihah)؛ استحفظته كذا أي سألته أن يحفظه عليك (ayn;sihah;tahdhib)؛ تفقد وتعهد ورعاية (mufradat)
- **B002** bellekte tutma — bir şeyi unutmayacak biçimde bellekte tutma · kitabı bölüm bölüm ezberledi
  الحفظ نقيض النسيان (ayn;tahdhib)؛ حفظته أيضا بمعنى استظهرته (sihah)؛ تحفظت الكتاب أي استظهرته (sihah)؛ رزقوا حفظ ما سمعوا وقلما ينسون (tahdhib)؛ هيئة النفس التي بها يثبت ما يؤدي إليه الفهم وضبط الشيء في النفس (mufradat)
- **B003** düzenli biçimde sürdürme — bir işi düzenli biçimde sürdürme · işi bırakmadan sürdürdü · işlerin üzerinde düzenli biçimde durma
  الحفاظ المحافظة على الأمور (maqayis)؛ المحافظة المواظبة على الأمور من الصلوات والعلم ونحوه (ayn)؛ المحافظة المواظبة على الأمر (tahdhib)؛ حافظ على الأمر والعمل وثابر عليه (tahdhib)
- **B004** uyanık ve dikkatli olma — hata yapmamak için tetikte ve dikkatli olma
  التحفظ قلة الغفلة (maqayis)؛ التحفظ قلة الغفلة حذرا من السقطة في الكلام والأمور (ayn)؛ التحفظ التيقظ وقلة الغفلة (sihah)؛ التحفظ قلة الغفلة في الكلام والتيقظ من السقطة (tahdhib)

- **B006** dokunulmazlıkları, sözleri ve bağlılığı koruma — yakınları, verilen sözü ve bağlılığı koruma · kendi topluluğunu arkadan kollayıp açıklarını örtenler · haksızlığa uğrayan yakını savunmanın eski kini giderdiğini anlatan söz · yokluğunda onu ve hakkını korudu · cinsel davranışta özdenetimini koruyanlar · eşleri yokken evlilik bağını gözeten kadınlar
  الحفاظ المحافظة على المحارم ومنعها عند الحروب (ayn)؛ أهل الحفائظ المحامون من وراء إخوانهم مانعون لعوراتهم (ayn)؛ حافظت على الرجل محافظة وحفاظا إذا حفظته في مغيبه (jamhara)؛ إن الحفائظ تنقض الأحقاد (jamhara;sihah)؛ الحفاظ المحافظة على العهد والمحاماة على الحرم (tahdhib)؛ الوفاء بالعقد والتمسك بالود (tahdhib)؛ فروجهم حافظون كناية عن العفة (mufradat)؛ حافظات للغيب أي يحفظن عهد الأزواج (mufradat)
- **B007** açık, düz ve kesintisiz yol [kalıp] — açık, düz ve kesintisiz süren yol

## ك ف ر (root_001307): 83:34 ٱلْكُفَّارِ, 83:36 ٱلْكُفَّارُ

- **B001** örtmek, kapatmak — bir şeyi örtmek ve kapatmak · zırhının üstüne bir giysi geçirmek · silahlarıyla örtünmek veya silah kuşanmak · rüzgârın savurduğu toprakla örtülmüş kül · güneşin yıldızları görünmez kılması

- **B003** dinî gerçeği reddetme — dinî gerçeği veya inancı reddetme · kalben bildiği gerçeği diliyle kabul etmeme · gerçeği bildiği hâlde inatla kabul etmemek · kalben reddederken diliyle inanmış görünmek · gerçeği hem kalple hem dille inkâr etmek

- **B004** nimeti yadsıma — nimeti yadsımak ve şükrünü yerine getirmemek · nimeti yadsıma ve şükretmeme · nimetleri aşırı biçimde yadsıyan kimse · iyilikleri karşılıksız ve teşekkürsüz kalan cömert adam
  كفران النعمة جحودها وسترها (maqayis)؛ الكفر نقيض الشكر كفر النعمة أي لم يشكرها (ayn)؛ الكفر أيضا جحود النعمة وهو ضد الشكر (sihah)؛ الكفر كفر النعمة وهو نقيض الشكر (tahdhib)؛ كفر النعمة وكفرانها سترها بترك أداء شكرها (mufradat)
- **B005** bağını reddedip uzaklaşmak — bir şeyle bağını reddedip ondan uzaklaşmak

- **B006** inançsız saymak — birini inançsız saymak veya öyle adlandırmak
  أكفرت الرجل أي دعوته كافرا لا تكفر أحدا (sihah)؛ أكفره إكفارا حكم بكفره (mufradat)
- **B007** itaatsizliğe zorlamak — itaat eden birini itaatsizliğe zorlamak
  إذا ألجأت مطيعك إلى أن يعصيك فقد أكفرته (ayn;tahdhib)
- **B008** tohumu örten çiftçi — tohumu toprakla örten çiftçi · tohumları toprakla örten çiftçiler
  يقال للزارع كافر لأنه يغطى الحب بتراب الأرض (maqayis)؛ الكافر الزارع لأنه يغطي البذر بالتراب (sihah)؛ الزراع لستره البذر في الأرض (mufradat)؛ الكفار الزراع (mufradat)
- **B009** günah yükünü giderme — günahı veya bozulan yeminin yükünü gideren karşılık · bozulan yeminin gerektirdiği yükümlülüğü yerine getirme · günahları örtüp etkisini silme
  الكفارة ما يكفر به من الخطيئة واليمين فيمحى به (ayn)؛ تكفير اليمين فعل ما يجب بالحنث فيها والاسم الكفارة والتكفير في المعاصي (sihah)؛ الكفارة ما يغطي الإثم والتكفير ستره وتغطيته حتى يصير بمنزلة ما لم يعمل (mufradat)
- **B010** çiçek veya meyve kılıfı — üzüm salkımının veya hurma çiçeğinin kılıfı · hurma çiçeğinin ya da meyvenin kılıfı · hurma ağacından çıkan kapalı çiçek kılıfları
  الكافور كم العنب قبل أن ينور وسمى كافورا لأنه كفر الوليع أي غطاه (maqayis)؛ الكافور كم العنب قبل أن ينور وكافوره ورقة الذي يستره والكافور الطلع والكفرى والكوافير (ayn)؛ الكافور الطلع ووعاء طلع النخل وكذلك الكفرى (sihah)؛ الكافور اسم أكمام الثمرة التي تكفرها والكافور أكمام الثمرة (mufradat)
- **B011** koku maddesi, su kaynağı veya bitki — güzel kokulu karışımlarda kullanılan madde · cennetteki bir su kaynağı · çiçeği papatyaya benzeyen bir bitki
  الكافور شيء من أخلاط الطيب والكافور عين ماء في الجنة والكافور نبات نوره كنور الأقحوان (ayn)؛ الكافور من الطيب (sihah)؛ الكافور الذي هو من الطيب (mufradat)
- **B012** uzak arazi; köy, uzak yer halkı veya mezar — insanlardan uzak, pek uğranmayan arazi · köy veya mezar · köyler veya uzak yerlerin halkı
  الكفر من الأرض ما بعد من الناس وأهل الكفور والقرى (maqayis)؛ الكافر من الأرض ما بعد عن الناس والكفور القرى (ayn)؛ الكفر أيضا القرية والكفر أيضا القبر (sihah)؛ الكافر من الأرض ما بعد عن الناس (tahdhib)
- **B013** dağ geçidi; iri dağ veya alçak duvar — dağ geçitleri · dağ geçidi veya iri dağ · alçak duvar
  الكفرات والكفر الثنايا من الجبال (maqayis)؛ الكفر الثنايا من الجبال (ayn)؛ الكفر العظيم من الجبال (sihah)؛ الكافر الحائط الواطىء (tahdhib)
- **B014** eğilerek boyun eğme gösterisi — başını eğmek veya elini göğsüne koyup eğilmek
  التكفير إيماء الذمي برأسه لا يقال سجد له وإنما يقال كفر له (ayn)؛ التكفير أن يخضع الإنسان لغيره يضع يده على صدره ويتطامن له (sihah)
- **B015** hükümdara taç giydirme veya taç — hükümdara taç giydirme veya tacın kendisi

## ث و ب (root_000209): 83:36 ثُوِّبَ

- **B001** önceki yere ya da duruma geri dönme — gittikten sonra geri dönmek; önceki yerine veya durumuna gelmek · insanların bir araya gelip toplanması · çekilen suyun geri gelmesi veya kabın yeniden dolması · insanların tekrar tekrar döndüğü yer · kuyu ağzında su çekenin durduğu yer veya suyun geri ulaştığı düzey · havuzda suyun geri dönüp toplandığı bölüm · birbirlerine doğru gelerek toplanan insan topluluğu · arıların geri döndüğü şey olarak bal
  الثاء والواو والباء قياس صحيح من أصل واحد وهو العود والرجوع (maqayis)؛ ثاب يثوب إذا رجع (maqayis)؛ وثاب يثوب ثوبا وثؤوبا إذا رجع وكل راجع ثائب (jamhara)؛ ثاب الرجل يثوب ثوبا وثوبانا رجع بعد ذهابه وثاب الناس اجتمعوا وجاءوا (sihah)؛ أصل الثوب رجوع الشيء إلى حالته الأولى (mufradat)؛ المثابة المكان يثوب إليه الناس (maqayis;sihah;mufradat)؛ ثاب الماء إذا بلغ إلى حاله الأولى بعد ما يستقى (jamhara)؛ ثبة الحوض ما يثوب إليه الماء (mufradat)
- **B002** yapılan işin sahibine dönen karşılık — yapılan işin karşılığı; çoğunlukla ödül · bir iş karşılığında verilen karşılık veya ödül · birine yaptığının karşılığını vermek · inanmayanlara yaptıklarının kötü karşılığını vermek
  الثواب من الأجر والجزاء أمر يثاب إليه (maqayis)؛ أعطيت فلانا ثوابه أي جزاء ما عمل (jamhara)؛ أثاب الله العباد يثيبهم إثابة وثوابا إذا جازاهم بأعمالهم (jamhara)؛ الثواب جزاء الطاعة وكذلك المثوبة (sihah)؛ هل ثوب الكفار ما كانوا يفعلون أي جوزوا (sihah)؛ الثواب ما يرجع إلى الإنسان من جزاء أعماله (mufradat)؛ الثواب يقال في الخير والشر لكن الأكثر المتعارف في الخير (mufradat)
- **B003** giysi — giysi · giysiler · giysi sözüyle kişinin kendisini dolaylı olarak anlatma
  الثوب الملبوس محتمل أن يكون من هذا القياس لأنه يلبس ثم يلبس ويثاب إليه (maqayis)؛ ربما عبروا عن النفس بالثوب (maqayis)؛ الثوب واحد الأثواب والثياب (sihah)؛ الثوب سمي بذلك لرجوع الغزل إلى الحالة التي قدرت له (mufradat)؛ وثيابك فطهر يحمل على تطهير الثوب وقيل الثياب كناية عن النفس (mufradat)
- **B004** çağrıyı yineleme — çağrıyı yineleme veya yeniden ibadete çağırma · sabah ibadet çağrısına, uykudan daha iyi olduğunu bildiren özel sözü ekleme · insanı tekrar tekrar etkileyen rahatsızlık
  التثويب الدعاء للصلاة وغيرها وأصله أن الرجل كان إذا جاء فزعا أو مستصرخا لوح بثوبه (jamhara)؛ صار يسمى الدعاء تثويبا (jamhara)؛ التثويب في أذان الفجر أن يقول الصلاة خير من النوم (sihah)؛ التثويب تكرار النداء ومنه التثويب في الأذان (mufradat)؛ الثوباء التي تعتري الإنسان سميت بذلك لتكررها (mufradat)
- **B005** evlilikte cinsel birliktelik yaşamış kişi — evlilikte cinsel birliktelik yaşamış erkek veya kadın; ayrıca eşinin yanından ayrılıp dönmüş kadın · kadınla evlilikte cinsel birliktelik kurup onu bu duruma getirmek
  رجل ثيب وامرأة ثيب الذكر والأنثى فيه سواء وذلك إذا كانت المرأة قد دخل بها أو كان الرجل قد دخل بامرأته (sihah)؛ الثيب التي تثوب عن الزوج (mufradat)

## ف ع ل (root_001167): 83:36 يَفْعَلُونَ

- **B001** bir şeyi yapıp etkileme — bir şeyi yapmak, ortaya çıkarmak veya üzerinde etki bırakmak · yapma işi, eylemin ad eylem biçimi · gerçekleşmiş işin veya eylemin adı · tek bir yapma olayı veya iyi ya da kötü iş · işlerin çoğulu · eylem adı olarak kullanılan biçim · bir etkinin altında değişmek veya ona uymak
  أصل صحيح يدل على إحداث شيء من عمل وغيره (maqayis)؛ فعل يفعل فعلا وفعلا فالفِعل المصدر والفِعل الاسم (ayn)؛ الفِعل بالفتح مصدر فعل يفعل والفِعل بالكسر الاسم (sihah)؛ فعلت الشيء فانفعل كسرته فانكسر (sihah)؛ فعل يفعل فعلا وفعلا فالمصدر مفتوح والاسم مكسور (tahdhib)
- **B002** iyi iş ve kişisel tutum — eli açıklık ve güzel davranış; ayrıca tek kişinin iyi ya da kötü işi
  الفَعال بفتح الفاء الكرم وما يفعل من حسن (maqayis)؛ الفَعال اسم للفعل الحسن مثل الجود والكرم ونحوه (ayn)؛ الفَعال بالفتح الكرم (sihah)؛ الفَعال فعل الواحد خاصة في الخير والشر (tahdhib)؛ الفَعال يكون في المدح والذم (tahdhib)
- **B003** el işçileri — işçiler, özellikle çamur ve kazı işlerinde çalışanlar · marangoz
  الفَعلة العملة وهم قوم يستعملون الطين والحفر وما يشبه ذلك من العمل (ayn)؛ الفَعلة قوم يعملون عمل الطين والحفر وما أشبه ذلك من العمل (tahdhib)؛ النجار يقال له فاعل (tahdhib)
- **B004** uydurup düzme — yalanı, düzme sözü veya anlatıyı uydurmak · sahibince ortaya atılmış veya önceki örneği olmadan yapılmış şey
  افتعل كذبا وزورا أي اختلق (sihah)؛ شعر مفتعل إذا ابتدعه قائله (tahdhib)؛ افتعل فلان حديثا إذا اخترقه (tahdhib)؛ يقال لكل شيء يسوى على غير مثال تقدمه مفتعل (tahdhib)
- **B005** karşılıklı eylem — eylem iki kişi arasında gerçekleştiğinde kullanılan ad
  الفِعال بكسر الفاء إذا كان الفعل بين الاثنين (tahdhib)؛ فإذا كان من فاعلين فهو فِعال (tahdhib)
- **B006** balta ya da keser sapı — balta sapı veya balta gözüne takılan ağaç parça; keser sapı
  يقولون الفَعال خشبة الفأس (maqayis)؛ الفَعال العود الذي يجعل في خرت الفأس يعمل به (tahdhib)؛ في نصاب القدوم سماه فَعالا (tahdhib)
- **B007** dil bilgisi tümleçleri — dil bilgisinde eyleme bağlanan öge türleri · eylemin yöneldiği veya etkilediği şey; nesne · eylemin yapılma amacı veya gerekçesi · yer, zaman veya durum bildirerek eyleme bağlanan öge · eylemin üzerinde gerçekleştiği alanı bildiren öge · doğrudan eylem adı; geçişli veya geçişsiz eylemle kullanılan tür
  المفعولات على وجوه في باب النحو (tahdhib)؛ فمفعول به (tahdhib)؛ ومفعول له (tahdhib)؛ ومفعول فيه (tahdhib)؛ ومفعول عليه (tahdhib)؛ ومفعول بلا صلة وهو المصدر (tahdhib)

## ECHO ء ب و (root_000007): for 83:36 يَفْعَلُونَ: withheld observed target; not identity

- **B001** babalık, besleyip yetiştirme ve oluşuma ya da iyileşmeye kaynaklık etme — baba · babalar, atalar ve baba yönünden onlara katılanlar · anne ile baba; bağlama göre baba ile amca veya dede · babalık veya baba soyu · birinin ya da bir topluluğun babası olmak · ebeveyn gibi besleyip büyütmek · birini baba edinmek · bir şeyin ortaya çıkmasına, düzelmesine veya görünür olmasına sebep olan kimse · konuklarla yakından ilgilenen kimse · savaşı kışkırtan kimse · bir kadının bekâretini bozan erkek
  يدل على التربية والغذو (maqayis)؛ أبوت الشيء آبوه أبوا إذا غذوته (maqayis)؛ فلان يأبو هذا اليتيم إباوة أي يغذوه كما يغذو الوالد ولده (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الأب أصله أبو (sihah)؛ الأب الوالد ويسمى كل من كان سببا في إيجاد شيء أو صلاحه أو ظهوره أبا (mufradat)
- **B002** babaya seslenme ve bağlama göre övgü ya da ağır yergi bildiren hitap kalıpları [kalıp] — babacığım diye seslenme · bağlama göre övgü ya da ağır sövgü bildiren hitap kalıbı · seni çekemeyenin babası olmasın anlamında onurlandırıcı hitap
  يا أبة افعل (sihah)؛ يا أبت ويا أبت لغتان (sihah)؛ لا أبا لك كأنه يمدحه (ayn)؛ لا أبا لك ولا أب لك مدح (sihah)؛ لا أبا لك ولا أب لك مدح ولا أم لك ذم (tahdhib)
- **B003** dağ keçisi idrarının kokusundan hastalanma — dağ keçisi idrarını koklayınca hastalanan dişi keçi · dağ keçisi idrarını koklayınca hastalanan erkek keçi
  عنز أبواء إذا أصابها وجع عن شم أبوال الأروى (maqayis)؛ عنز أبواء وتيس آبى إذا شم بول الأروى فمرض منه (sihah)



===== _commentary/v16/work/s083/surah.r2/channels.md =====
(source: latent_activation/network/v3/reviews/s083/reader_a_pilot.md)

# S083 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. Moral Boundary, Repair, and Returned Judgment
- Semantic invariant: Conduct is measured by whether it preserves or crosses a moral limit, then returns to the actor as blame, repair, or recompense.
- Surface relation: direct; 83:7 `فُجَّارِ`, 83:10-12 forms of `ك ذ ب`, 83:12 `أَثِيمٍ` and `مُعْتَدٍ`, 83:18/22 `أَبْرَار`, 83:29 `أَجْرَمُ`, and 83:36 `ثُوِّبَ`.
- Surprising reach: The same cycle extends backward to delay before good and forward to confession, renunciation, and expiation.

#### Subchannel A. Truth Enacted as Recognized Good
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A truthful deed becomes socially and morally recognizable as goodness, obedience, and beneficence.
- Active motifs: enacted truth `ب ر ر:B001/m01`; expansive good and obedience `ب ر ر:B002/m01`; recognized good `ع ر ف:B005/m01`; willing obedience `د ي ن:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ب ر ر` 83:18,22; `ع ر ف` 83:24; `د ي ن` 83:11.
- Synthesis: Truth is not only a proposition here but conduct that proceeds into action. Its social face is the good that sound judgment recognizes, while its relational form is willing obedience.

#### Subchannel B. Sin, Crime, and Transgression
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: An actor crosses a limit, turns breach into an offense, and bears the identity and consequence of wrongdoing.
- Active motifs: punishable sin `ء ث م:B003/m01`; crime or culpable offense `ج ر م:B004/m01`; crossing the just limit `ع د و:B001/m01`; violation of right and restraint `ف ج ر:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ث م` 83:12; `ج ر م` 83:29; `ع د و` 83:12; `ف ج ر` 83:7.
- Synthesis: The motifs form a single moral sequence: breach of a boundary becomes a defined offense, the offense identifies its agent, and culpability makes punishment intelligible.

#### Subchannel C. Abstention, Renunciation, and Expiation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A wrongdoer withdraws from sin, disavows the offense, and covers its remaining liability through expiation.
- Active motifs: refraining from sin `ء ث م:B002/m01`; renunciation of implicated conduct `ك ف ر:B005/m01`; covering or erasing sin by expiation `ك ف ر:B009/m01`; return to broad good `ب ر ر:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ث م` 83:12; `ك ف ر` 83:34,36; `ب ر ر` 83:18,22.
- Synthesis: Abstention begins the repair, renunciation separates the actor from the wrong, and expiation addresses what remains. The scene closes by re-entry into active good rather than mere nonparticipation.

#### Subchannel D. Imputation, Suspicion, and Confession
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Guilt is placed upon someone, entertained as an accusation, denied or admitted, and converted into accountable speech.
- Active motifs: loading sin onto another `ء ث م:B004/m01`; accusatory suspicion `ظ ن ن:B004/m01`; charging someone with falsehood `ك ذ ب:B002/m01`; confession and acknowledgment `ع ر ف:B009/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ث م` 83:12; `ظ ن ن` 83:4; `ك ذ ب` 83:10-12,17; `ع ر ف` 83:24.
- Synthesis: Imputation supplies the charge, suspicion supplies its uncertain social circulation, and confession reverses that uncertainty by making responsibility explicit.

#### Subchannel E. Moral Delay and Abandonment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Good is not rejected outright but deferred through slackness until prior companionship or obligation is left behind.
- Active motifs: lagging behind good `ء ث م:B001/m01`; slackness and failure to exert oneself `ء ل ي:B008/m01`; delay and detention `ء ل ي:B013/m01`; leaving after accompaniment `ت ل و:B005/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ث م` 83:12; `ء ل ي` 83:31 `إِلَىٰ`; `ت ل و` 83:13.
- Synthesis: The scene captures a quieter moral failure than open transgression. Delay becomes a mode of neglect, and prolonged neglect ends in abandonment of what had once been followed.

#### Subchannel F. Recompense Returning to Its Source
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A deed reaches its outcome, returns to its doer as payment, and fixes the final moral condition.
- Active motifs: recompense returning to the worker `ث و ب:B002/m01`; reckoning and requital `د ي ن:B002/m01`; return to final outcome `ء و ل:B002/m01`; reversal and return `ق ل ب:B005/m01`; falling into ruin `و ي ل:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ث و ب` 83:36; `د ي ن` 83:11; `ء و ل` 83:13; `ق ل ب` 83:14,31; `و ي ل` 83:1,10.
- Synthesis: Recompense is figured as a return rather than an external addition. The deed travels toward its end, turns back upon its source, and resolves either as due payment or ruin.

### 2. Measure, Exchange, and Liability
- Semantic invariant: Goods, values, and obligations are made commensurable so that transfer can be completed without hidden shortfall.
- Surface relation: direct; 83:1-3 names deficient measuring, taking in full, weighing, and causing loss.
- Surprising reach: Physical measure opens into market demand, pricing, debt, cash, surety, and delegated equivalence.

#### Subchannel A. Fair Measure Against Concealed Shortfall
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A quantity is measured, checked against justice, and either delivered in full or reduced to another party's loss.
- Active motifs: deficient measure `ط ف ف:B003/m01`; measuring food `ك ي ل:B001/m01`; loss through measure and weight `خ س ر:B003/m01`; just balance `و ز ن:B002/m01`; completion without deficiency `و ف ي:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ط ف ف` 83:1; `ك ي ل` 83:2,3; `خ س ر` 83:3; `و ز ن` 83:3; `و ف ي` 83:2.
- Synthesis: The measuring instrument mediates between the parties. Justice requires that the measured amount and the delivered amount coincide; any hidden reduction converts exchange into injury.

#### Subchannel B. Brim, Fullness, and the Last Increment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A vessel or quantity approaches its rim, is compared with a nearby amount, and passes from near-full to complete.
- Active motifs: the rim of a measure `ط ف ف:B002/m01`; overflowing fullness `ض ح ك:B008/m01`; approximation in amount `ق ر ب:B016/m01`; encompassing totality `ك ل ل:B003/m01`; complete fulfillment `و ف ي:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ط ف ف` 83:1; `ض ح ك` 83:29,34; `ق ر ب` 83:21,28; `ك ل ل` 83:12; `و ف ي` 83:2.
- Synthesis: These motifs isolate the boundary at which a small increment matters. The brim marks a measurable threshold, approximation brings the amount close, and fulfillment removes the residual gap.

#### Subchannel C. Market Bargain, Demand, and Price
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Buyers and sellers negotiate an item while demand establishes its market standing and price.
- Active motifs: market demand and briskness `ق و م:B018/m01`; bargaining over an affair `ق و ل:B009/m01`; comparison between alternatives `ك ي ل:B005/m01`; valuation and pricing `ق و م:B010/m01`; commercial loss `خ س ر:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ق و م` 83:6; `ق و ل` 83:13,17,32; `ك ي ل` 83:2,3; `خ س ر` 83:3.
- Synthesis: Negotiation tests alternatives, demand gives the item a market position, and pricing turns that position into an exchange value. A failed bargain or adverse market converts value into commercial loss.

#### Subchannel D. Debt, Cash, Credit, and Earning
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Present money is distinguished from deferred liability, a loan advances resources, and earning supplies repayment.
- Active motifs: financial debt `د ي ن:B003/m01`; cash on hand `ع ي ن:B011/m01`; advance or loan `ع ي ن:B012/m01`; earning livelihood and gain `ك س ب:B001/m01`; transferring benefit to another `ك س ب:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `د ي ن` 83:11; `ع ي ن` 83:28; `ك س ب` 83:14.
- Synthesis: Cash and credit occupy different points in one transfer cycle. The loan moves value before repayment, while earning restores the debtor's capacity to discharge the deferred claim.

#### Subchannel E. Surety, Pledge, and Dependent Burden
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A guarantor stands behind a measured obligation, a pledge follows its holder, and liability carries a dependent weight.
- Active motifs: personal surety `ك و ن:B003/m01`; a right or pledge following its owner `ت ل و:B004/m01`; measured food as the secured object `ك ي ل:B001/m01`; dependent burden `ك ل ل:B002/m01`; custody of an entrusted object `ح ف ظ:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ك و ن` 83:14,17,29,36; `ت ل و` 83:13; `ك ي ل` 83:2,3; `ك ل ل` 83:12; `ح ف ظ` 83:33.
- Synthesis: The obligation is made portable by attaching it to persons and things. The pledge follows its owner, the surety stands in reserve, and the dependent burden names what must still be carried.

#### Subchannel F. Equivalence, Alignment, and Appraisal
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Two things are placed in correspondence, aligned, and judged for equality, substitution, or relative worth.
- Active motifs: balancing two things in alignment `و ز ن:B003/m01`; equal measured amount `ق و م:B015/m01`; matching like with like `ن ظ ر:B006/m01`; acting as another's equivalent or proxy `ق و م:B007/m01`; comparative appraisal `ك ي ل:B005/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `و ز ن` 83:3; `ق و م` 83:6; `ن ظ ر` 83:23,35; `ك ي ل` 83:2,3.
- Synthesis: Equivalence is produced by a procedure, not assumed. Alignment makes differences visible, appraisal tests them, and substitution becomes legitimate only when the compared positions are made commensurate.

#### Subchannel G. Precious Good, Competition, and Hoarding
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A scarce valued object attracts competing claimants, rises in demand, and may be withheld rather than circulated.
- Active motifs: precious object competed for `ن ف س:B010/m01`; market demand `ق و م:B018/m01`; hoarding wealth `م س ك:B002/m01`; earning or acquisition `ك س ب:B001/m01`; present cash `ع ي ن:B011/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن ف س` 83:26; `ق و م` 83:6; `م س ك` 83:26; `ك س ب` 83:14; `ع ي ن` 83:28.
- Synthesis: Preciousness generates desire, competition intensifies demand, acquisition transfers control, and hoarding interrupts recirculation of the valued resource.

### 3. Temporal Course, Occurrence, and Terminal Change
- Semantic invariant: Events occupy a bounded course with a beginning, recurrence, delay, approach, and completed outcome.
- Surface relation: direct; 83:4-6,11,34 repeatedly anchors a coming day, rising, and temporal reckoning.
- Surprising reach: Ordinary duration expands into respite, cyclical passage, catastrophic occurrence, and resurrection.

#### Subchannel A. Beginning, Endpoint, and Completion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A process starts, advances toward its limit, and is brought to a completed close.
- Active motifs: first beginning `ء و ل:B001/m01`; endpoint and termination `ء ل ي:B001/m01`; reaching the final part `خ ت م:B001/m01`; complete fulfillment `و ف ي:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء و ل` 83:13; `ء ل ي` 83:31 `إِلَىٰ`; `خ ت م` 83:25,26; `و ف ي` 83:2.
- Synthesis: The temporal line is framed from both ends. Beginning establishes priority, the endpoint supplies direction, and completion certifies that no unfinished remainder persists.

#### Subchannel B. Duration, Passage, and Recurrence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Time is experienced as a bounded day, an extended period, a repeated occurrence, and a passage that eventually cuts off.
- Active motifs: bounded daytime `ي و م:B001/m01`; duration or period `ي و م:B002/m01`; temporal recurrence `م ر ر:B002/m01`; occurrence in time `ك و ن:B001/m01`; completed and severed duration `ج ر م:B006/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ي و م` 83:5,6,11,34; `م ر ر` 83:30; `ك و ن` 83:14,17,29,36; `ج ر م` 83:29.
- Synthesis: Recurrence gives duration internal rhythm, while occurrence gives each passage a definite presence. Completion closes the period by cutting it off from what follows.

#### Subchannel C. Waiting, Respite, and Imminence
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A claimant waits, an interval opens, and the awaited event moves from deferment into nearness.
- Active motifs: waiting and delay `ء ي ي:B001/m01`; granting respite `ن ظ ر:B002/m01`; breadth of time and interval `ن ف س:B015/m01`; approaching expiration `ق ر ب:B002/m01`; detained delay `ء ل ي:B013/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ي ي` 83:13; `ن ظ ر` 83:23,35; `ن ف س` 83:26; `ق ر ب` 83:21,28; `ء ل ي` 83:31 `إِلَىٰ`.
- Synthesis: Respite is a structured interval between demand and event. Waiting occupies the interval, nearness contracts it, and expiration ends the possibility of further deferment.

#### Subchannel D. Calamitous Event and Rising
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A severe occurrence arrives as the event of a day, rouses what was still, and brings a collective rising.
- Active motifs: the day's calamitous occurrence `ي و م:B003/m01`; an adverse state coming to pass `ك و ن:B006/m01`; bitter ordeal `م ر ر:B004/m01`; overwhelming calamity `ع ظ م:B004/m01`; resurrection and rising `ق و م:B013/m01`; rousing from stillness `ب ع ث:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ي و م` 83:5,6,11,34; `ك و ن` 83:14,17,29,36; `م ر ر` 83:30; `ع ظ م` 83:5; `ق و م` 83:6; `ب ع ث` 83:4.
- Synthesis: Event, severity, and rising form one eschatological mechanism. The day is not a neutral date but an occurrence that breaks stasis, gathers bodies into standing, and fixes an adverse or saving outcome.

### 4. Record, Inscription, and Authentication
- Semantic invariant: An event or identity is made durable and publicly recoverable through writing, marking, enrollment, and sealing.
- Surface relation: direct; 83:7-9 and 83:18-21 repeat `كِتَاب` and `مَرْقُوم`, while 83:25-26 repeats sealing.
- Surprising reach: Documentary fixation extends into animal brands, terrain marks, banners, rosters, and binding decrees.

#### Subchannel A. Written Line and Numbered Record
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Characters are arranged into a line and fixed as a legible, numbered record.
- Active motifs: written text `ك ت ب:B002/m01`; numbered inscription `ر ق م:B001/m01`; aligned written line `س ط ر:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ك ت ب` 83:7,9,18,20; `ر ق م` 83:9,20; `س ط ر` 83:13.
- Synthesis: Joining letters creates the written object, alignment makes it readable, and numbering or inscription gives the record a stable identity that can be consulted again.

#### Subchannel B. Seal, Impression, and Secure Closure
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A completed record or vessel receives an identifying impression and is closed against alteration.
- Active motifs: seal impression `خ ت م:B002/m01`; secure sealing that prevents access `خ ت م:B003/m01`; inscribed line beneath the seal `ر ق م:B001/m01`; terminal completion `خ ت م:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `خ ت م` 83:25,26; `ر ق م` 83:9,20.
- Synthesis: Completion and authentication coincide. The impression identifies what has been closed, while the secured closure preserves the integrity of the content.

#### Subchannel C. Decree, Enrollment, and Roster
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A name is entered into a register and the inscription acquires binding legal force.
- Active motifs: binding written determination `ك ت ب:B003/m01`; enrollment in a register or class `ك ت ب:B004/m01`; reckoning attached to the decree `د ي ن:B002/m01`; affirmative confirmation `ن ع م:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ك ت ب` 83:7,9,18,20; `د ي ن` 83:11; `ن ع م` 83:22,24.
- Synthesis: Enrollment locates a person within an ordered group, and the written determination turns classification into consequence. Confirmation closes the administrative act.

#### Subchannel D. Visible Mark, Brand, and Landmark
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A visible trace distinguishes an animal, object, route, or place and guides later recognition.
- Active motifs: decorative or visible pattern `ر ق م:B003/m01`; branded traces on an animal `ر ق م:B004/m01`; terrain trace or named landmark `ر ق م:B005/m01`; distinguishing sign `ع ل م:B002/m01`; witnessing emblem `ش ه د:B008/m01`; raised banner `ر ء ي:B011/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ر ق م` 83:9,20; `ع ل م` 83:6; `ش ه د` 83:21; `ر ء ي` 83:32.
- Synthesis: A mark converts surface into memory. On bodies it identifies ownership or condition, on land it preserves location, and as a banner it enlarges the same identifying function for a group.

### 5. Voice, Report, and Contested Claim
- Semantic invariant: Speech moves from bodily articulation through response and circulation to negotiation, fabrication, or public attribution.
- Surface relation: direct; 83:13,17,32 repeatedly stages saying, recitation, denial, and attributed judgment.
- Surprising reach: The speech field includes vocal texture, alternating voices, rumor in a settlement, bargaining, and fabricated narrative.

#### Subchannel A. Articulation and the Body of Voice
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Breath and tongue produce an audible utterance whose clarity and volume give speech a bodily presence.
- Active motifs: spoken utterance `ق و ل:B001/m01`; tongue as speech instrument `ق و ل:B002/m01`; articulate mobility of the tongue `ب ل ل:B006/m01`; body and projection of voice `ج ر م:B008/m01`; vocal clamor `ب ر ر:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ق و ل` 83:13,17,32; `ب ل ل` 83:14 `بَلْ`; `ج ر م` 83:29; `ب ر ر` 83:18,22.
- Synthesis: The tongue shapes the utterance, breath projects it, and vocal body determines whether it emerges as clear speech or clamor. The scene joins anatomy to communicative force.

#### Subchannel B. Repeated Call and Answering Voice
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A call is repeated, another voice follows it, and the exchange becomes correspondence or response.
- Active motifs: reiterated call `ث و ب:B004/m01`; one voice following another `ت ل و:B007/m01`; reciprocal correspondence `ر س ل:B008/m01`; responsive affirmation `ه ا ء:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ث و ب` 83:36; `ت ل و` 83:13; `ر س ل` 83:33; `ه ا ء` 83:17,32.
- Synthesis: Repetition keeps the summons active until an answering voice enters. Following, replying, and corresponding turn isolated sound into a relational exchange.

#### Subchannel C. Rumor, Banter, and Public Reputation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A report circulates among people, becomes entertaining talk, and affects a person's reputation.
- Active motifs: widely circulating report `ق و ل:B007/m01`; witty or entertaining talk `ف ك ه:B003/m01`; talk that consumes reputations `ف ك ه:B007/m01`; remote village as circulation setting `ك ف ر:B012/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ق و ل` 83:13,17,32; `ف ك ه` 83:31; `ك ف ر` 83:34,36.
- Synthesis: Circulation detaches speech from its first speaker. Banter makes it socially attractive, while talk about others turns entertainment into reputational consumption across the settlement.

#### Subchannel D. False Attribution and Fabricated Narrative
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A speaker invents a claim, attributes it to another, and gives the invention the shape of a received story.
- Active motifs: false attribution to another `ت ل و:B009/m01`; saying what did not occur `ق و ل:B005/m01`; direct falsehood `ك ذ ب:B001/m01`; charging another with lying `ك ذ ب:B002/m01`; invented narrative `ف ع ل:B004/m01`; false tales `س ط ر:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ت ل و` 83:13; `ق و ل` 83:13,17,32; `ك ذ ب` 83:10-12,17; `ف ع ل` 83:36; `س ط ر` 83:13.
- Synthesis: Fabrication supplies the invented content, attribution relocates its source, and narration gives the result enough form to circulate as inherited story rather than obvious invention.

#### Subchannel E. Deliberation, Bargaining, and Adjudicative Speech
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Participants exchange views, compare positions, resist one another, and authorize a practical judgment.
- Active motifs: negotiation over an affair `ق و ل:B009/m01`; comparative bargaining `ك ي ل:B005/m01`; reciprocal deliberation `ن ظ ر:B010/m01`; resistance or contest `ق و م:B014/m01`; imposed ruling `ق و ل:B010/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ق و ل` 83:13,17,32; `ك ي ل` 83:2,3; `ن ظ ر` 83:23,35; `ق و م` 83:6.
- Synthesis: Deliberation opens positions to comparison, bargaining tests their practical terms, and resistance clarifies the dispute. Adjudicative speech ends the exchange by imposing an operative result.

### 6. Reference, Relation, and Discourse Commitment
- Semantic invariant: Linguistic operators locate a referent, join it to another term, open or correct discourse, and bind speakers to propositions.
- Surface relation: indirect; interrogative and demonstrative forms occur at 83:8,13,17,19,26,32, while 83:14 uses `بَلْ` and 83:31 uses `إِلَىٰ`.
- Surprising reach: Function words form scenes of pointing, questioning, oath-taking, correction, essence-definition, and even letter and rhyme construction.

#### Subchannel A. Question, Demonstration, and Attention
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A speaker directs attention to a referent, asks which or what it is, and frames the inquiry as a presentation.
- Active motifs: interrogative selection `ء ي ي:B004/m01`; demonstrative pointing `ذ و و:B003/m01`; interrogative or relative “what” `ذ و و:B004/m01`; attention-opening particle `ه ا ء:B002/m01`; presentational inquiry `ر ء ي:B013/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ي ي` 83:13; `ذ و و` 83:17,26; `ه ا ء` 83:17,32; `ر ء ي` 83:32.
- Synthesis: Pointing establishes a candidate referent, inquiry requests its specification, and the attention marker makes the referent jointly available to speaker and hearer.

#### Subchannel B. Joining, Accompaniment, and Relative Attachment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: One expression is attached to another so that accompaniment, possession, or relative dependence can be read as a single construction.
- Active motifs: accompaniment and inclusion `ء ل ي:B003/m01`; joining one item to another `ك ت ب:B001/m01`; relative attachment `ذ و و:B002/m01`; relation expressed by an “on/above” preposition `ع ل و:B012/m01`; relational particle `ر ب ب:B015/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ل ي` 83:31 `إِلَىٰ`; `ك ت ب` 83:7,9,18,20; `ذ و و` 83:17,26; `ع ل و` 83:18,19; `ر ب ب` 83:6,15.
- Synthesis: Joining supplies structural cohesion, while the relational terms specify how the joined expressions depend on one another. The construction moves from mere adjacency to interpretable accompaniment or possession.

#### Subchannel C. Affirmation, Oath, and Pledge
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A proposition is affirmed, introduced under oath, and made binding through a pledge that follows its bearer.
- Active motifs: affirmative answer `ن ع م:B004/m01`; oath-opening affirmation `ء ي ي:B010/m01`; swearing an oath `ء ل ي:B007/m01`; following pledge or obligation `ت ل و:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن ع م` 83:22,24; `ء ي ي` 83:13; `ء ل ي` 83:31 `إِلَىٰ`; `ت ل و` 83:13.
- Synthesis: Affirmation accepts the proposition, the oath heightens commitment to it, and the pledge gives that commitment a durable relation to the person responsible.

#### Subchannel D. Correction, Resumption, and Responsive Assent
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Discourse interrupts a prior inference, redirects it, and resumes with an explicit answer.
- Active motifs: corrective resumption `ب ل ل:B008/m01`; responsive assent `ه ا ء:B003/m01`; affirmative answer `ن ع م:B004/m01`; statement presented with conjectural force `ق و ل:B011/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ب ل ل` 83:14 `بَلْ`; `ه ا ء` 83:17,32; `ن ع م` 83:22,24; `ق و ل` 83:13,17,32.
- Synthesis: Correction blocks the prior trajectory without ending discourse. The response then supplies a new commitment, and the conjectural statement marks the proposition being revised or accepted.

#### Subchannel E. Definition, Essence, and Signification
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A thing is isolated as itself, assigned a limit or definition, and allowed to signify its condition.
- Active motifs: essence or selfsame identity `ن ف س:B012/m01`; the thing itself `ع ي ن:B013/m01`; defining limit `ق و ل:B016/m01`; a thing's indicative “saying” `ق و ل:B014/m01`; locus where a quality is expected `ظ ن ن:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن ف س` 83:26; `ع ي ن` 83:28; `ق و ل` 83:13,17,32; `ظ ن ن` 83:4.
- Synthesis: Identity supplies the referent, definition gives it a boundary, and signification lets its state communicate beyond explicit speech. The locus motif grounds abstract definition in where a property is expected to reside.

#### Subchannel F. Letter, Pronunciation, and Rhyme
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A named letter is pronounced and positioned so that it can govern the terminal sound of a verse or utterance.
- Active motifs: the letter hāʾ `ه ا ء:B006/m01`; directing the rhyme ending `و ج ه:B011/m01`; articulate tongue `ب ل ل:B006/m01`; written line `س ط ر:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ه ا ء` 83:17,32; `و ج ه` 83:24; `ب ل ل` 83:14 `بَلْ`; `س ط ر` 83:13.
- Synthesis: The letter supplies the phonetic unit, articulation realizes it, and rhyme-direction fixes its position at a formal boundary. Writing gives the same ordered sound a visual line.

#### Subchannel G. Vocative Address and Pronominal Focus
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A speaker calls a near or distant addressee and fixes that person as the focused object of warning or presentation.
- Active motifs: near or distant vocative `ء ي ي:B008/m01`; emphatic pronominal support `ء ي ي:B005/m01`; presentational “take” particle `ه ا ء:B001/m01`; demonstrative pointing `ذ و و:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ي ي` 83:13; `ه ا ء` 83:17,32; `ذ و و` 83:17,26.
- Synthesis: The vocative opens contact, the pronoun fixes the addressee's grammatical role, and presentation makes the pointed object or warning immediate.

#### Subchannel H. Enumeration and Explanatory Expansion
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An expression introduces a large count or gathered plurality and then expands it through an explanatory gloss.
- Active motifs: indefinite large number `ء ي ي:B007/m01`; explanatory “that is” `ء ي ي:B009/m01`; numerous gathered groups `ر ب ب:B004/m01`; grouped multitudes `ك ل ل:B009/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ي ي` 83:13; `ر ب ب` 83:6,15; `ك ل ل` 83:12.
- Synthesis: Enumeration opens an unspecified plurality, grouped nouns give it collective form, and explanatory expansion clarifies what the count or group comprises.

### 7. Trust, Worship, and Ritual Response
- Semantic invariant: Inner assent becomes embodied observance, invocation, offering, and communal response.
- Surface relation: direct; belief at 83:29,34, standing at 83:6, nearness at 83:21,28, and judgment at 83:11 organize the surface contrast.
- Surprising reach: The devotional field includes the spoken “amen,” rain prayer, repeated call, pilgrimage standing, and ostentatious performance.

#### Subchannel A. Security, Assent, and Entrusted Belief
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: The heart becomes secure, accepts a proposition, and entrusts itself to what it affirms.
- Active motifs: settled security of heart `ء م ن:B001/m01`; trusted assent `ء م ن:B002/m01`; belief and delegation `د ي ن:B007/m01`; affirmative confirmation `ن ع م:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء م ن` 83:29,34; `د ي ن` 83:11; `ن ع م` 83:22,24.
- Synthesis: Security removes destabilizing fear, assent accepts the claim, and delegation places practical reliance upon it. Affirmation voices the inner settlement.

#### Subchannel B. Standing Observance and Prayer
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A worshiper assumes the bodily posture of prayer and maintains the practice through repeated observance.
- Active motifs: prescribed prayer `ص ل ي:B001/m01`; standing posture `ق و م:B002/m01`; persevering observance `ح ف ظ:B003/m01`; obedient submission `د ي ن:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ص ل ي` 83:16; `ق و م` 83:6; `ح ف ظ` 83:33; `د ي ن` 83:11.
- Synthesis: Posture embodies worship, while preservation through repetition turns a single act into observance. Obedience supplies the relation that makes the posture devotional rather than merely physical.

#### Subchannel C. Invocation, Rain Prayer, and Amen
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A community calls for mercy or rain, repeats the summons, and answers the petition with “amen.”
- Active motifs: supplication and blessing `ص ل ي:B002/m01`; prayer for rain `س ق ي:B006/m01`; repeated summons `ث و ب:B004/m01`; “amen” seeking response `ء م ن:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ص ل ي` 83:16; `س ق ي` 83:25; `ث و ب` 83:36; `ء م ن` 83:29,34.
- Synthesis: Supplication supplies the request, rain prayer gives it a concrete communal need, repetition keeps the appeal active, and “amen” converts listeners into participants seeking fulfillment.

#### Subchannel D. Offering, Sacred Nearness, and Pilgrimage Standing
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A worshiper approaches through an offering, joins a witnessed sacred standing, and enters a favored proximity.
- Active motifs: offering that brings nearness `ق ر ب:B005/m01`; pilgrimage standing at ʿArafah `ع ر ف:B007/m01`; witnessed presence `ش ه د:B001/m01`; privileged nearness `ق ر ب:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ق ر ب` 83:21,28; `ع ر ف` 83:24; `ش ه د` 83:21.
- Synthesis: The offering is the approach mechanism, standing is the embodied ritual setting, and witnessed presence confirms participation. Nearness becomes both spatial movement and conferred status.

#### Subchannel E. Ostentatious Devotion
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A devotional act is redirected toward human observation and converted into reputation.
- Active motifs: acting to be seen `ر ء ي:B005/m01`; supplication as the displayed act `ص ل ي:B002/m01`; circulating public report `ق و ل:B007/m01`; visible presentation `ر ء ي:B012/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ر ء ي` 83:32; `ص ل ي` 83:16; `ق و ل` 83:13,17,32.
- Synthesis: The act retains a devotional form but changes audience. Display solicits human notice, and circulating report supplies the social reward that displaces the original addressee.

#### Subchannel F. Submission, Humility, and Worshipful Rule
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person lowers resistance, accepts rule, and turns obedience into worshipful submission.
- Active motifs: yielding submission `ك و ن:B004/m01`; bodily humility `ك ف ر:B014/m01`; obedience `د ي ن:B001/m01`; acknowledged lordship `ر ب ب:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ك و ن` 83:14,17,29,36; `ك ف ر` 83:34,36; `د ي ن` 83:11; `ر ب ب` 83:6,15.
- Synthesis: Humility changes posture, obedience changes conduct, and acknowledged lordship supplies the relation toward which both are directed.

### 8. Authority, Custody, and Legal Status
- Semantic invariant: Social order is maintained by recognized office, guarded covenant, adjudication, and lawful change of status.
- Surface relation: indirect; 83:6,15 names lordship, 83:33 guardianship, and 83:7-20 repeatedly joins record with judgment.
- Surprising reach: Authority reaches from ruler, headman, and chamberlain to lost-property procedure, inheritance exclusion, proxy, bondage, and manumission.

#### Subchannel A. Ruler, Steward, Headman, and Chamberlain
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A ruler authorizes speech and delegates daily governance to stewards, local heads, and access officers.
- Active motifs: dominion and lordship `ر ب ب:B001/m01`; political management `ء و ل:B004/m01`; authoritative speaker or prince `ق و ل:B004/m01`; custodial governance `ق و م:B004/m01`; headman who knows the group `ع ر ف:B006/m01`; chamberlain controlling access `ح ج ب:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ر ب ب` 83:6,15; `ء و ل` 83:13; `ق و ل` 83:13,17,32; `ق و م` 83:6; `ع ر ف` 83:24; `ح ج ب` 83:15.
- Synthesis: Sovereignty supplies the source of authority, the steward maintains affairs, the headman mediates local knowledge, and the chamberlain governs physical access to the center.

#### Subchannel B. Watch, Covenant, and Protected Trust
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A covenant creates a protected obligation and assigns watchers to preserve it against breach.
- Active motifs: guarding an entrusted object `ح ف ظ:B001/m01`; preserving covenant and sanctity `ح ف ظ:B006/m01`; covenant held in trust `ر ب ب:B011/m01`; vigilant watcher `ن ظ ر:B009/m01`; supervisory control `س ط ر:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ح ف ظ` 83:33; `ر ب ب` 83:6,15; `ن ظ ر` 83:23,35; `س ط ر` 83:13.
- Synthesis: The covenant defines what must be preserved, custody assigns responsibility, and surveillance makes preservation continuous rather than episodic.

#### Subchannel C. Lost Property, Public Identification, and Redress
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A stray animal or found object is announced publicly, identified by description, and restored through adjudicative redress.
- Active motifs: stray livestock without a known owner `ض ل ل:B005/m01`; public identification of lost property `ع ر ف:B008/m01`; appeal to authority for redress `ع د و:B005/m01`; imposed ruling `ق و ل:B010/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ض ل ل` 83:32; `ع ر ف` 83:24; `ع د و` 83:12; `ق و ل` 83:13,17,32.
- Synthesis: Loss creates uncertain ownership, public description tests claims, and adjudication restores the object by linking recognition to an enforceable remedy.

#### Subchannel D. Bondage, Installment Contract, and Manumission
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A subjugated person enters a written installment contract, earns toward its terms, and returns to free status.
- Active motifs: enslavement and subjugation `د ي ن:B004/m01`; manumission contract `ك ت ب:B005/m01`; earning toward discharge `ك س ب:B001/m01`; return to a former or new condition `ث و ب:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `د ي ن` 83:11; `ك ت ب` 83:7,9,18,20; `ك س ب` 83:14; `ث و ب` 83:36.
- Synthesis: Bondage supplies the initial legal condition, writing specifies staged payment, earning performs the contract, and return names the status change achieved at completion.

#### Subchannel E. Inheritance, Collateral Kin, and Exclusion
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A decedent's estate is distributed among kin while collateral relation and exclusion rules determine shares.
- Active motifs: collateral kinship and inheritance `ك ل ل:B004/m01`; family line returning to a household `ء و ل:B003/m01`; exclusion from an allotted share `ح ج ب:B008/m01`; recognized notables or brothers `ع ي ن:B015/m01`; binding apportionment `ك ت ب:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ك ل ل` 83:12; `ء و ل` 83:13; `ح ج ب` 83:15; `ع ي ن` 83:28; `ك ت ب` 83:7,9,18,20.
- Synthesis: Kinship defines eligible relations, the decree assigns portions, and exclusion modifies those portions when a nearer or qualifying relation intervenes.

#### Subchannel F. Delegation, Proxy, and Standing in Another's Place
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: One person is appointed to occupy another's legal or practical position under a rule of equivalence.
- Active motifs: acting in another's place `ق و م:B007/m01`; position or station `ك و ن:B002/m01`; matching an equivalent `ن ظ ر:B006/m01`; alignment between representatives `و ز ن:B003/m01`; custodial mandate `ق و م:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ق و م` 83:6; `ك و ن` 83:14,17,29,36; `ن ظ ر` 83:23,35; `و ز ن` 83:3.
- Synthesis: Delegation transfers function without erasing the original position. Equivalence legitimizes substitution, while mandate constrains what the proxy may do.

### 9. Household, Kinship, and Social Belonging
- Semantic invariant: People become socially located through family relation, marriage, shared habitation, hospitality, fellowship, and rank.
- Surface relation: indirect; 83:2,6 addresses people, 83:21,28 names the near ones, and 83:31 depicts return to one's household.
- Surprising reach: Household belonging reaches into collateral kin, step-relations, courtship, greeting, benefaction, and privileged intimacy.

#### Subchannel A. Family Line and Collateral Relation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person belongs to a household through descent, return, collateral relation, and enduring social attachment.
- Active motifs: household affiliation `ء ه ل:B001/m01`; family line and dependents `ء و ل:B003/m01`; blood kinship `ق ر ب:B003/m01`; collateral kin `ك ل ل:B004/m01`; social tie that holds people together `م س ك:B009/m02`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ه ل` 83:31; `ء و ل` 83:13; `ق ر ب` 83:21,28; `ك ل ل` 83:12; `م س ك` 83:26.
- Synthesis: Descent supplies the close line, collateral kin extends it laterally, and the holding tie makes family a durable social relation rather than a list of names.

#### Subchannel B. Marriage, Courtship, and Step-Relation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A household is formed through marriage, courtship changes a woman's status, and children enter step-relations.
- Active motifs: taking a spouse `ء ه ل:B002/m01`; woman approached in courtship `ر س ل:B009/m01`; previously married woman `ث و ب:B005/m01`; stepchild or child reared in another household `ر ب ب:B005/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ه ل` 83:31; `ر س ل` 83:33; `ث و ب` 83:36; `ر ب ب` 83:6,15.
- Synthesis: Courtship initiates the transition, marriage establishes the household, prior marital status shapes the union, and nurture incorporates a child whose relation is social as well as genealogical.

#### Subchannel C. Human Fellowship and Community
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Human presence overcomes estrangement and develops into companionship, sociability, and an assembled community.
- Active motifs: humanity opposed to wild isolation `ء ن س:B001/m01`; companionship removing loneliness `ء ن س:B003/m01`; relaxed sociability `ر س ل:B007/m01`; gathered people `ق و م:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ن س` 83:2,6 `ٱلنَّاس`; `ر س ل` 83:33; `ق و م` 83:6.
- Synthesis: Recognition of another human first removes fear, repeated presence becomes companionship, and sociability scales that relation into a community.

#### Subchannel D. Welcome, Gift, and Welfare
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A host receives another with spacious welcome, gives material benefit, and turns generosity into durable welfare.
- Active motifs: expansive greeting and welcome `ء ه ل:B005/m01`; gift or bestowal `ء ل ي:B012/m01`; benefaction and social support `ب ل ل:B002/m01`; kindness to kin `ب ر ر:B003/m01`; abundant generosity `ف ج ر:B005/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ه ل` 83:31; `ء ل ي` 83:31 `إِلَىٰ`; `ب ل ل` 83:14 `بَلْ`; `ب ر ر` 83:18,22; `ف ج ر` 83:7.
- Synthesis: Welcome opens social space, the gift fills it with a concrete transfer, and sustained benefaction converts a single reception into welfare and kinship care.

#### Subchannel E. Notables, Favorites, and Social Prestige
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A group distinguishes its notables and grants favored persons proximity, visibility, and honor.
- Active motifs: elite notables `ع ي ن:B015/m01`; favored intimates of authority `ق ر ب:B004/m01`; social prestige `و ج ه:B006/m01`; honor and sanctity `ع ظ م:B010/m01`; chosen intimate or confidant `ء ن س:B006/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ع ي ن` 83:28; `ق ر ب` 83:21,28; `و ج ه` 83:24; `ع ظ م` 83:5; `ء ن س` 83:2,6 `ٱلنَّاس`.
- Synthesis: Selection raises certain persons from the wider group, proximity materializes favor, and public prestige makes the resulting hierarchy visible.

### 10. Mockery, Exposure, and Social Reversal
- Semantic invariant: Social judgment is enacted through covert gesture, laughter, fault-finding, shame, and eventual reversal of who observes whom.
- Surface relation: direct; 83:29-36 stages laughter, winking, amused return, accusation, observation, and reversed laughter.
- Surprising reach: The scene extends from a private eyelid signal to public reputation, bodily expression, shamelessness, and returned humiliation.

#### Subchannel A. Covert Gesture and Fault-Finding
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Members of a group identify a target, signal one another covertly, and turn a suspected defect into shared ridicule.
- Active motifs: covert wink or hand signal `غ م ز:B002/m01`; locating a defect or point of attack `غ م ز:B003/m01`; accusatory suspicion `ظ ن ن:B004/m01`; visible effect of a disparaging look `ن ظ ر:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `غ م ز` 83:30; `ظ ن ن` 83:4; `ن ظ ر` 83:23,35.
- Synthesis: The signal recruits an audience without open speech, suspicion supplies a fault, and the repeated look marks the target socially and visibly.

#### Subchannel B. Laughter, Joke, and Amused Talk
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Facial expression identifies an object of laughter and develops into shared amusement and witty talk.
- Active motifs: smiling or laughing face `ض ح ك:B001/m01`; object made laughable `ض ح ك:B002/m01`; delight or astonishment expressed as laughter `ض ح ك:B003/m01`; witty banter `ف ك ه:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ض ح ك` 83:29,34; `ف ك ه` 83:31; `و ج ه` 83:24.
- Synthesis: Expression makes amusement visible, the laughable target gives it direction, and banter sustains it as a group activity rather than a momentary reaction.

#### Subchannel C. Shameless Pride and Public Insolence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Social restraint falls away, pride hardens into insolence, and the actor displays contempt before others.
- Active motifs: shamelessness `ج ح م:B005/m01`; insolent exultation `ف ك ه:B006/m01`; boastful self-magnification `ع ظ م:B006/m01`; arrogant elevation `ع ل و:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ج ح م` 83:16; `ف ك ه` 83:31; `ع ظ م` 83:5; `ع ل و` 83:18,19.
- Synthesis: Pride supplies the inward valuation, elevation externalizes hierarchy, and shamelessness removes the restraint that would otherwise limit public contempt.

#### Subchannel D. Returned Derision and Disgrace
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: The social direction of mockery reverses, and the former observers become the object of laughter and public disgrace.
- Active motifs: reversal of position `ق ل ب:B004/m01`; recompense returned to the actor `ث و ب:B002/m01`; object of laughter `ض ح ك:B002/m01`; lament of disgrace `و ي ل:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ق ل ب` 83:14,31; `ث و ب` 83:36; `ض ح ك` 83:29,34; `و ي ل` 83:1,10.
- Synthesis: Reversal changes the direction of the gaze, recompense changes the ownership of the act, and laughter exposes those who had treated exposure as their privilege.

### 11. Sight, Appearance, and Epistemic Access
- Semantic invariant: Knowledge depends on what can be seen, displayed, recognized, inferred, concealed, or watched.
- Surface relation: direct; seeing at 83:32, looking at 83:23,35, recognition at 83:24, witnessing at 83:21, and supposition at 83:4 form an explicit epistemic field.
- Surprising reach: Visual knowledge reaches into eye anatomy, reflected human images, dream, apparition, harmful gaze, espionage, divination, and omen-bearing birds.

#### Subchannel A. Looking, Display, and Mutual Facing
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: An observer directs sight toward a displayed object while participants face one another and share a visible scene.
